([sananthanarayan/skilldrop](https://github.com/sananthanarayan/skilldrop/tree/HEAD/packs/ai-engineering/skills/agent-loop-design) · ★2)
- **agent-loop-design**: Design an autonomous, policy-driven agent loop — covering loop pattern, phases, guardrails, HITL checkpoints, tool contracts, and observability — for a given workflow or governance objective. ([fabianmagrini/skills](https://github.com/fabianmagrini/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-loop-design) · ★1)
- **agent-loop-design**: Use when structuring an agentic system's core loop — plan→act→observe→ reflect phases, exit conditions, state carried between iterations — before writing the agent code. Triggers: "design an agent loop", "structure this agent", "my agent wanders off task", "agentic workflow architecture", "plan-act-observe", "when should the agent stop". ([Nandansai08/skillz](https://github.com/Nandansai08/skillz/tree/HEAD/skills/loop-engineering/agent-loop-design) · ★1)
- **agent-loop-design**: Use when turning repeated agent work into a loop with trigger conditions, evidence, stopping criteria, uninstall state, validation checks, and reviewable artifacts. ([sebastienrousseau/agtmls](https://github.com/sebastienrousseau/agtmls/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-loop-design) · ★1)
- **agent-loop-design**: Use when building bounded autonomous coding-agent loops, maker-checker workflows, multi-step orchestration, retries, checkpoints, or human-in-the-loop approval flows. ([laxmi-narayan-87/plugins](https://github.com/laxmi-narayan-87/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/harness-engineering/skills/agent-loop-design))
- **agent-loop-designer**: Design single-agent or multi-agent workflows, orchestration loops, handoffs, evaluator-optimizer cycles, and wake-up or re-entry patterns for agentic systems. Use when Codex needs to decide whether work should stay as a prompt, instruction, skill, single agent, or multi-agent topology, and when turning that choice into concrete prompt contracts, prompt-refinement gates, externalized state schemas, stop rules, approval gates, and close-loop evaluation plans.
